Game:  The game is a variant of Nim where players take turns removing 1 to 3 items from a single pile, and the player who takes the last item wins.
Strategy:  To win the game, always leave a multiple of 4 items for your opponent after your turn. If it's your turn and the pile has 1 to 3 items, take all of them to win. If there are 4 or more items, calculate the number of items to take (1 to 3) such that the remaining items are a multiple of 4.

The next multiple of 4 below 8 is 4, so I can consider leaving 4 items by taking 1 item (8 - 1 = 7) or taking 2 items (8 - 2 = 6) or taking 3 items (8 - 3 = 5). All of these options do not leave a multiple of 4 for the opponent. However, I can remove 3 items to leave 5, which does not provide a winning position for the opponent either. Therefore, I will take 3 items to maximize my winning chances in future turns.
